2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]

2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]     途中に表を1つ挿しただけで以降が全部ずれた（Xiaomi の表に Kimi の行が入った）。

2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]     for n, m in enumerate(marks):
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]         anchor = m.group(1)
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]         seg_end = marks[n + 1].start() if n + 1 < len(marks) else len(html)
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]         seg = html[m.start():seg_end]
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]         t = TABLE_RE.search(seg)
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]         if not t:
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]             continue
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]         spans.setdefault(anchor, (m.start() + t.start(), m.start() + t.end(), seg))
2026-11-26 05:15:34 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c]     return spans

That reading makes "料金表は5列まで" consistent for OpenAI.

2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] # More ways to move care forward with ChatGPT for Healthcare
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Introducing computer-use agents, a new healthcare model configuration, and new healthcare plugins that work across healthcare software to get real work done.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] [Contact our sales team](https://openai.com/contact-sales/)
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Listen to article7:45
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Share
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Healthcare teams need to piece together information from the EHR, hospital policies, scheduling systems, and payer portals to deliver care and manage operations. With ChatGPT for Healthcare, we’re bringing the tools teams already use and the medical evidence they need into one platform to help them spend less time navigating systems and more time caring for patients.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Today, we’re sharing advances in computer use that help AI agents carry out multi-step work across existing healthcare software. We’re also improving how trusted clinical search finds, evaluates, and cites medical evidence. A new healthcare model configuration in the Responses API lets developers build trusted clinical search into their own applications. And we’re expanding our healthcare plugin ecosystem with more than 20 new plugins.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Together, these capabilities help healthcare teams find the information they need, make decisions grounded in medical evidence, and carry out next steps across the systems they already use.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] ## Carry work forward across healthcare systems with computer-use agents
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Organizations often need to move information between disconnected systems to prepare documentation, coordinate care, and complete administrative tasks. Computer-use agents can help with these steps by navigating the software that teams already use: reading screens, clicking, typing, and moving between applications.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Today, we’re sharing advances in computer-use agents for healthcare, including more capable, safe, and affordable models for clinical and administrative workflows, and access controls and audit logs for secure deployments. We’re also sharing our work with healthcare organizations that are deploying agents, including Abridge, Ambience, Cigna Healthcare, and CVS Health, to process referrals, move documents between systems, and bring together the information care teams need. In the demo below, a clinician uses Voice in the ChatGPT app to ask for help following up on an MRI request. The agent reviews the chart and drafts an order and patient reply for review.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] A clinician uses voice to ask an agent to review a chart and draft an MRI order and patient reply for review.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 00:00
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Healthcare organizations can start with a workflow that spans existing applications, set which applications agents can access, and establish clear controls for which steps require human review. To support HIPAA-compliant workflows involving protected health information, organizations must use eligible services covered by their BAA with OpenAI, apply required security and retention settings, and extend protections to connected systems. Content shared with ChatGPT for Healthcare is not used to train models.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Read more about our computer use research, customer stories, and demonstrations in [Advancing computer-use agents for healthcare teams⁠](https://openai.com/index/advancing-computer-use-for-healthcare-teams/).
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] === bench ===
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 60:GPT‑6.2 Astra with the healthcare configuration ranked first among the systems we evaluated on HealthBench Professional, a benchmark of 525 challenging tasks written by physicians to test how well AI can answer questions about patient care, draft clinical documentation, and synthesize medical evidence. It earned a length-adjusted score of 74.4%, 6.3 percentage points ahead of the highest-scoring non-OpenAI system, Claude Fable 5.2 with web search.

On HealthBench Professional, GPT‑6.2 Astra configured for healthcare delivered higher-quality responses in an average of 23 seconds, compared with nearly three minutes for the same model using general-purpose web search even when using its highest reasoning setting.
2026-11-26 05:16:14 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 70:For developers deploying these tools at scale, cost per response matters too. In a separate comparison on the same benchmark, Astra with the healthcare configuration scored higher than Claude Fable 5.2 with web search at less than half the API cost per scored response ($1.37 vs. $2.90), with both systems at their highest reasoning settings.

2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 59 In a standard Transformer, each layer runs once per forward pass. 2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 67 When converting middle layers of an existing model into a multi-pass loop, directly overwriting hidden states causes activation norms to blow up across iterations. Downstream layers then receive representations outside their expected scale and distribution.GLM-5.4 adds independent per-token gates for each iteration to proportionally write candidate updates back into the residual stream. After trai
2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 68 
2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 69 ###### Hybrid KV Cache (Global Sharing + Independent Sliding Window)
2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 70 
2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 71 A naive implementation keeps a full KV cache for every loop iteration, multiplying memory usage with the loop count. Instead, we build on cross-loop KV sharing by supplementing it with a local sliding window for the new pass: the second loop reuses the sparse KV selected in the first pass, while retaining its own newly generated KV within the sliding window. These two KV streams can overlap at the
2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 72

2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 74 
2026-11-26 05:23:40 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 75 GLM-5.4’s base model uses sparse attention. The second loop directly reuses the attention indices generated by the first loop’s indexer, adding zero indexing overhead. Our experiments show this performs nearly identically to recomputing indices on every pass.

2026-11-26 05:24:12 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 95 '<p>On Nov 23, 2026 Z.ai released <strong>GLM-5.4</strong>, the newest flagship of the GLM family. It is the first model built on a <strong>Loop Transformer</strong>: a shared block of layers runs for multiple iterations, adding reasoning depth without adding parameters. GLM-5.4 loops 28 layers of the GLM-5 base twice and, at 750B scale, reaches what Z.ai describes as intelligence comparable to 2T\u20133T parameter models. Context length is 1M tokens.</p>'
2026-11-26 05:24:12 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 97 '<p><strong>Pricing status:</strong> as of November 24, 2026 the docs.z.ai pricing table lists no per-token price for GLM-5.4. It is available through the <strong>GLM Coding Plan</strong> and chat.z.ai, and Z.ai\'s blog lists the API as "Coming Soon". We will add API pricing to this table and to the <a href="/en/pricing/">pricing comparison</a> as soon as Z.ai publishes it.</p>'

2026-11-26 05:24:57 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 'ot xai"></span>xAI</span><span class="legend-item"><span class="legend-dot meta"></span>Meta</span></div>\n<div class="tl-wrap">\n<div class="year-divider">2026</div>\n<article class="tl-item dot-other" id="glm-5-4"><div class="tl-card"><div class="tl-date">2026-11-23 <span class="tl-provider-tag" style="background:rgba(167,139,250,.15);color:var(--c-accent2);">Zhipu / Z.ai</span></div><h2 class="tl-title" id="sec-62">GLM-5.4 released \u2014 the first Loop Transformer flagship, usable on GLM Coding Plan and chat.z.ai (API Coming Soon)</h2><p class="tl-desc">Z.ai&rsquo;s official post (23 Nov 2026) is headlined &ldquo;GLM-5.4: Toward Generalized Coding with Our First Loop Transformer&rdquo;. It is a 750B model that loops 28 GLM-5 layers twice &mdash; the Loop Transformer &mdash; targeting 2T-3T-class reasoning without adding parameters, built by continued training from the existing GLM-5 base. The model docs list <strong>a 1M context window and up to 128K output</strong>, with video, image, text and file input. It is usable through the GLM Coding Plan, ZCode and Z.ai Chat. The API is <strong>not yet available</strong>: the post lists &ldquo;API (Coming Soon)&rdquo; and the docs add &ldquo;The Model API will be made available upon completion of the security assessment.&rdquo; Re-checked on 26 Nov 2026, the pricing page (docs.z.ai) still carries a lastModified of 23 Nov 2026 and lists only GLM-5.3, GLM-5.3-Flash and GLM-5.3-FlashX under Latest Models, so <strong>neither a GLM-5.4 row nor a rate is published</strong>. Weights are planned for open release after safety hardening (&ldquo;We plan to open-source the model weights responsibly after completing necessary safety hardening.&rdquo;).</p><div class="tl-links"><a href="https://z.ai/blog/glm-5.4" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Official announcement &rarr;</a><a href="https://docs.z.ai/guides/overview/pricing" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Pricing page &rarr;</a><a href="/en/glm-5-3-flash/">Compared with GLM-5.3-Flash &rarr;</a></div></div></article>\n<article class="tl-item dot-google" id="gemini-4-neon"><div class="tl-card"><div class="tl-date">2026-11-18 <span class="tl-provider-tag google">Google</span></div><h2 class="tl-title" id="sec-60">Gemini 4 Neon now generally available (GA) \u2014 $3.00 / $15.00 flat across the full 1M tokens</h2><p class="tl-desc">The official Gemini API release notes (November 18, 2026) state, verbatim: &ldquo;Gemini 4 Neon (gemini-4-neon) generally available (GA)&rdquo; and &ldquo;$3.00 per 1M input tokens and $15.00 per 1M output tokens&rdquo;. It supports four thinking levels (low, medium, default high, xhigh), a <strong>unified 1M-token context window</strong> and long decode up to 1M tokens. The pricing page lists $3.00 input / $15.00 output (including thinking tokens) and $0.15 context caching, <strong>implicit caching only</strong> (explicit caching is not supported), with <strong>no free tier</strong>. No tiered pricing above 200K tokens is listed.<span class="tl-price-badge">$3.00 / $15.00</span></p><div class="tl-links"><a href="https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/changelog" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Release notes &rarr;</a><a href="https://ai.google.dev/gemi'

2026-11-26 05:28:33 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Best approach: add a `date_label` field support to og_card.py? I could modify /app/og_card.py to draw `spec.get("date_label") or spec["generated"]`. That's a code change in /app which is the deployed app image — may or may not persist across restarts. 2026-11-26 05:28:33 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Alternative cleaner: render with an in-memory spec copy where generated="2026-11-15", then save og_specs with generated="2026-11-26". 2026-11-26 05:28:33 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] However there's a subtlety: if I write `generated: 2026-11-26` and burned date drawn as 2026-11-15 from an ephemeral copy, any future regeneration via og_card.py (render_page) would burn 2026-11-26 and re-create mismatch. Better to add a persistent field to the spec such as `"date_label": "2026-11-15"` and make og_card.py honor it. But is that field's presence going to break page_audit? 2026-11-26 05:31:10 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] Lessons to add:
2026-11-26 05:31:10 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 1. file_read mode=search は正規表現の `|`（alternation）を解釈しない。「A|B」で0件ならパターンを分けて引き直す。
2026-11-26 05:31:10 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 2. og_specs.json のキーはサイドパス形式（"image-api-pricing/index.html"）。grep は "image-api" のような部分文字列で。
2026-11-26 05:31:10 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 3. OG画像の日付: og_card.py は既定で「描き直した日」を焼くので、本文の最終更新日と違うときは as_of を使う（描き直し日≠画像の日付）。
2026-11-26 05:31:10 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 4. 画像だけの更新では render_site を通さない（FEATURE_PAGES の本文が再生成される）。site_upload で対象のみ。
2026-11-26 05:31:10 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 5. エンジニアは DONE の後に通称を書く（`DONE: gpt-6-2-healthcare`）。依頼文の冒頭に `item_id` を明記し、「最終行は `DONE: <id>` だけ、通称や説明を書くな」と書く。
2026-11-26 05:31:10 JST [akira/akira/c36d1ebad5234b2686b3bbe61b615d1c] 6. 業種別構成（for Law / for Healthcare）は独立モデルIDとして台帳に持つが、年表は1件に集約・計算機には入れない（今回確立）。
